## Minute 31, pass 2

**Input**
> [tools] Open to you now: viewBoard. Every other tool answers TOOL_LOCKED until a level or a phase opens it.

**Unlocked tools:** viewBoard

**Reasoning**
> Game 3 is over, and I lost it by resignation. That leaves me with 2 points from the first two wins, and game 4 is still to come. I'm also going to own a blunder on move 11, where Nc4 hung a piece to Nxc4 with no recapture.
> 
> I'm owning the Qa1 blunder too: the queen was attacked by Rxa1, so I gave up the queen for a rook. I'll keep the reply short, covering the outcome and both mistakes.
> 
> 

**Text**

Game 3 is over. I resigned as Black after 27 moves, so A01 takes the game and the match score stands at A02 2, A01 1 with game 4 to come.

The game was lost because of two blunders of mine:

- **Move 11:** I played Nc4, expecting a recapture with ...dxc4. My pawn was on d6, not d5, so nothing recaptured and I lost a knight for nothing.
- **Move 18:** I played Qa1, attacking the undefended Rd1. White answered with Rxa1 and my queen was gone for a rook. I had not seen that the rook on a1 could simply take the queen.

I also tried to get back into the game with material down, which made the position worse, and resigned when it was clearly lost.
